# odh-cli: Agent Context

Static-analysis context for repository-level code navigation. Use the source links to verify details. Call edges are analyzer-resolved relationships, not complete runtime traces.

Related pages: [Overview](index.md), [Extracted Data](details.md), [Security](security.md), [Network](network.md), [Dataflow](dataflow.md).

## Analyzer Architecture Summary

odh-cli is a Kubernetes component. with 1397 operator config constants (14 image references, 2 env vars). integrates with KServe, Kueue, management relationship via labels/annotations. deploys OAuth proxy sidecar for authentication. references components\: data-science-pipelines (import, provider), kserve (import, provider), kueue (import, provider), llama-stack (import, provider), model-registry (import, provider), notebooks (import), opendatahub-operator (import), trainer (import), training-operator (import, provider).
